## 分析完成总结

### 22 个 >=60d gap Fork 分类

| 类别 | 数量 | Fork |
|------|------|------|
| **纯同步型**（无自定义） | 5 | manmen-mi、shaneholloman、nagyist、frankbria、scope-impact |
| **轻度自定义** | 8 | ysamlan、mikesigs、shangobashi、w4sp0、maeste、jhfnetboy、0xzapata |
| **⚡ 有实质自定义开发** | 7 | cicero-im、principal-forks、sh5080、Myran、eisbaw、jammus、hanbyul-kim、anmoa、adriannoes |
| **pjsample** | 1 | 与 sh5080 同源，轻度自定义 |

### 关键发现

**1. 两个主要的自定义开发集群：**

- **TASK-XXX 集群**（cicero-im、sh5080、Myran、eisbaw、jammus、hanbyul-kim、anmoa、adriannoes、pjsample）：这些 fork 使用了 `TASK-XXX` 编号体系而非上游的 `BACK-XXX`，说明它们形成了自己的任务管理生态。其中：
  - **cicero-im**：Mermaid 图表渲染、NixOS 支持、基线构建目标、Shell tab 补全
  - **sh5080/pjsample**：TUI+Web 多项改进、agent 指令优化
  - **Myran**：Claude PR Assistant 工作流、零填充 ID、看板自动更新
  - **eisbaw**：Nix 打包重构（bun2nix）、统计概览、git hooks 绕过
  - **jammus/hanbyul-kim/anmoa/adriannoes**：任务类型字段、Web UI 里程碑页、标签过滤

- **BACK-XXX 但有自定义**（principal-forks、ysamlan、mikesigs、shangobashi、w4sp0、maeste、jhfnetboy、0xzapata、frankbria、scope-impact）：保留上游编号体系，但有自己的改进：
  - **principal-forks**：最重量级的自定义，OpenTelemetry 仪器化，6 个核心领域的 canvas 事件文件，最近 5 个 commit 改了 299 个文件
  - **ysamlan/0xzapata/mikesigs**：符号链接遍历、小写 ID 规范化
  - **shangobashi/frankbria/scope-impact**：AGENTS.md 更新、动画替换
  - **w4sp0/maeste/jhfnetboy**：TPM skill 更新、tmp skill

**2. 纯同步型 fork 占约 23%**（5/22），说明大部分长期活跃的 fork 都有一定程度的自定义。

**3. 报告已更新**：所有 22 个 fork 的 `Analyzed` 列已从 `>=60d` 改为具体的分析摘要。

     1	import React, { useMemo, useState } from "react";
     2	import type { Task } from "../../types";
     3	
     4	interface GanttTask {
     5		task: Task;
     6		id: string;
     7		title: string;
     8		status: string;
     9		plannedStart: Date;
    10		plannedEnd: Date;
    11	}
    12	
    13	function toDate(value: string): Date | null {
    14		if (!value) return null;
    15		const hasTime = value.includes(" ") || value.includes("T");
    16		const iso = value.replace(" ", "T") + (hasTime ? ":00Z" : "T00:00:00Z");
    17		const d = new Date(iso);
    18		return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;
    19	}
    20	
    21	type ZoomLevel = "1d" | "7d" | "30d" | "90d" | "365d" | "all";
    22	
    23	interface GanttPageProps {
    24		tasks: Task[];
    25		onEditTask?: (task: Task) => void;
    26	}
    27	
    28	export const GanttPage: React.FC<GanttPageProps> = ({ tasks, onEditTask }) => {
    29		const [zoom, setZoom] = useState<ZoomLevel>("30d");
    30		const ganttTasks: GanttTask[] = useMemo(() => {
    31			return tasks
    32				.map((task) => {
    33					const start = task.plannedStart ? toDate(task.plannedStart) : null;
    34					const end = task.plannedEnd ? toDate(task.plannedEnd) : null;
    35					if (!start && !end) return null;
    36					const normalizedStart = start ?? end;
    37					const normalizedEnd = end ?? start;
    38					if (!normalizedStart || !normalizedEnd) return null;
    39					if (normalizedEnd.getTime() < normalizedStart.getTime()) return null;
    40					return {
    41						task,
    42						id: task.id,
    43						title: task.title,
    44						status: task.status,
    45						plannedStart: normalizedStart,
    46						plannedEnd: normalizedEnd,
    47					};
    48				})
    49				.filter((t): t is GanttTask => Boolean(t));
    50		}, [tasks]);
    51	
    52		const fullRange = useMemo(() => {
    53			if (ganttTasks.length === 0) return null;
    54			let min = ganttTasks[0]!.plannedStart.getTime();
    55			let max = ganttTasks[0]!.plannedEnd.getTime();
    56			for (const task of ganttTasks) {
    57				const start = task.plannedStart.getTime();
    58				const end = task.plannedEnd.getTime();
    59				if (start < min) min = start;
    60				if (end > max) max = end;
    61			}
    62			// Add small padding
    63			const dayMs = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    64			return { min: min - dayMs, max: max + dayMs };
    65		}, [ganttTasks]);
    66	
    67		const dayMs = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    68	
    69		const range = useMemo(() => {
    70			if (!fullRange) return null;
    71			const spanMs = fullRange.max - fullRange.min;
    72	
    73			// "All" shows the full task planning range
    74			if (zoom === "all") {
    75				const span = Math.max(spanMs, dayMs);
    76				return { min: fullRange.min, max: fullRange.max, span };
    77			}
    78	
    79			// Other zoom levels anchor relative to "today"
    80			const windowDays = zoom === "1d" ? 1 : zoom === "7d" ? 7 : zoom === "30d" ? 30 : zoom === "90d" ? 90 : 365;
    81			const windowMs = windowDays * dayMs;
    82	
    83			const now = Date.now();
    84			const start = now - dayMs; // 1 day before today
    85			const min = start;
    86			const max = min + windowMs;
    87			const span = Math.max(max - min, dayMs);
    88			return { min, max, span };
    89		}, [fullRange, zoom]);
    90	
    91		const formatDay = (d: Date, showYear = false) =>
    92			d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
    93				year: showYear ? "numeric" : undefined,
    94				month: "short",
    95				day: "numeric",
    96			});
    97	
    98		const ticks = useMemo(() => {
    99			if (!range) return [];
   100			const out: { time: number; label: string }[] = [];
   101	
   102			// Short windows: daily ticks
   103			if (zoom === "1d" || zoom === "7d") {
   104				const step = dayMs;
   105				const start = Math.floor(range.min / step) * step;
   106				for (let t = start; t <= range.max + 1; t += step) {
   107					out.push({ time: t, label: formatDay(new Date(t), false) });
   108				}
   109				return out;
   110			}
   111	
   112			// 30d window: weekly ticks (start-of-week)
   113			if (zoom === "30d") {
   114				const step = 7 * dayMs;
   115				const startDate = new Date(range.min);
   116				startDate.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
   117				// Align to Monday (0=Sunday,...,6=Saturday)
   118				const day = startDate.getDay();
   119				const diffToMonday = (day + 6) % 7; // 0 if Monday, 1 if Tuesday, etc.
   120				startDate.setDate(startDate.getDate() - diffToMonday);
   121	
   122				for (let t = startDate.getTime(); t <= range.max + 1; t += step) {
   123					out.push({ time: t, label: formatDay(new Date(t), false) });
   124				}
   125				if (out.length === 0) {
   126					out.push({ time: range.min, label: formatDay(new Date(range.min), false) });
   127				}
   128				return out;
   129			}
   130	
   131			// Longer windows: month-aligned ticks
   132			const startDate = new Date(range.min);
   133			startDate.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
   134			startDate.setDate(1); // start of month
   135	
   136			const endDate = new Date(range.max);
   137			endDate.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
   138	
   139			const showYear = zoom === "365d" || zoom === "all";
   140	
   141			for (let d = new Date(startDate); d <= endDate; d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + 1)) {
   142				out.push({ time: d.getTime(), label: formatDay(d, showYear) });
   143			}
   144	
   145			if (out.length === 0) {
   146				out.push({ time: range.min, label: formatDay(new Date(range.min), showYear) });
   147			}
   148	
   149			return out;
   150		}, [range, zoom]);
   151	
   152		return (
   153			<div className="container mx-auto px-4 py-8 transition-colors duration-200">
   154				<div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
   155					<h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-white">Gantt Timeline</h1>
   156					<p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">

   193										onClick={() => setZoom(value)}
   194										className={`px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium border-l border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 first:border-l-0 transition-colors duration-150 ${

   215	
   216						{/* Simple time axis aligned with task rows */}
   217						<div className="relative h-8 mb-2">
   218							<div className="flex items-center gap-3 h-full">

   220								<div className="w-56" />

   225									<div className="absolute inset-x-0 top-0 h-full">
   226										{ticks.map((tick) => {
   227											const rawOffset =
   228												range.span > 0 ? ((tick.time - range.min) / range.span) * 100 : 0;
   229											const clampedOffset = Math.min(Math.max(rawOffset, 0), 100);
   230	
   231											let translate = "-50%";
   232											if (clampedOffset === 0) {
   233												translate = "0%";
   234											} else if (clampedOffset === 100) {
   235												translate = "-100%";
   236											}
   237	
   238											return (
   239												<div
   240													key={tick.time}
   241													className="absolute -top-1 text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400"
   242													style={{
   243														left: `${clampedOffset}%`,
   244														transform: `translateX(${translate})`,
   245													}}
   246												>
   247													{tick.label}
   248												</div>
   249											);
   250										})}
   251									</div>
   252								</div>
   253							</div>
   254						</div>
   255	
   256						<div className="space-y-2">
   257							{ganttTasks.map((task) => {
   258								const startMs = task.plannedStart.getTime();
   259								const endMs = task.plannedEnd.getTime();
   260	
   261								const clampedStart = Math.max(startMs, range.min);
   262								const clampedEnd = Math.min(endMs, range.max);
   263	
   264								if (clampedEnd <= range.min || clampedStart >= range.max) {
   265									return null;
   266								}
   267	
   268								const startOffset = ((clampedStart - range.min) / range.span) * 100;
   269								const width = Math.max(((clampedEnd - clampedStart) / range.span) * 100, 2);
   270	
   271								const status = (task.status || "").toLowerCase();
   272								const color =
   273									status.includes("done") || status.includes("complete")
   274										? "bg-green-500 dark:bg-green-400"
   275										: status.includes("progress")
   276											? "bg-blue-500 dark:bg-blue-400"
   277											: "bg-gray-400 dark:bg-gray-500";
   278	
   279								return (
   280									<div
   281										key={task.id}
   282										className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer"
   283										onClick={() => {
   284											if (onEditTask) {
   285												onEditTask(task.task);
   286											}
   287										}}
   288									>
   289										<div className="w-56 truncate text-sm text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">
   290											<span className="font-mono text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mr-2">
   291												{task.id}

   293											{task.title}

   297											<div
   298												className={`absolute top-0 bottom-0 rounded-full ${color} shadow-sm`}
   299												style={{
   300													left: `${startOffset}%`,
   301													width: `${width}%`,
   302												}}
   303												title={`${formatDay(task.plannedStart)} → ${formatDay(task.plannedEnd)}`}
   304											/>
   305										</div>
   306									</div>
   307								);
   308							})}
   309						</div>
   310					</div>
   311				)}
   312			</div>
   313		);

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

The user wants me to study andrewlongman07's Gantt chart implementation in detail and document the technical approach in the report. I now have a comprehensive understanding of the implementation:

1. **Data model**: Added `plannedStart` and `plannedEnd` fields to Task, TaskCreateInput, TaskUpdateInput types
2. **Markdown persistence**: Uses `planned_start` / `planned_end` frontmatter keys, parsed/serialized via normalizeDate
3. **CLI**: Added `--planned-start` and `--planned-end` options to task create/edit commands
4. **Core**: Added date field handling in Core.createTask and Core.updateTask
5. **Server API**: Passes planned dates through create/update endpoints
6. **Web UI - TaskDetailsModal**: Added date input fields with inline update (optimistic, websocket-driven refresh)
7. **Web UI - GanttPage**: 
   - Pure React implementation (no external charting library)
   - CSS-based bar positioning (left offset + width percentages)
   - Zoom levels: 1d, 7d, 30d, 90d, 365d, all
   - Smart tick generation (daily for short windows, weekly for 30d, monthly for longer)
   - Status-colored bars (green=done, blue=in-progress, gray=default)
   - Click to edit task
   - Tasks without dates are hidden with a helpful empty state
8. **Navigation**: Added Gantt link in SideNavigation with custom SVG icon
9. **Routing**: Added `/gantt` route in App.tsx

The implementation is elegant in its simplicity - no D3, no charting library, just pure React + CSS positioning. The bars are positioned using percentage-based left offset and width within a relative container.

Let me also check the test file to see what was tested.

</details>

Deep Dive: andrewlongman07 — Gantt Timeline & Scheduling\n\n**Fork:** [andrewlongman07/Backlog.md](https://github.com/andrewlongman07/Backlog.md)  \n**Created:** 2025-12-08 | **Last pushed:** 2025-12-17 | **Gap:** 9 days  \n**Key feature:** Planned start/end dates + browser Gantt chart view\n\n### 3.1 Overview\n\nThis fork adds **project planning capabilities** to Backlog.md through two new task fields (`plannedStart` / `plannedEnd`) and a fully client-side **Gantt timeline view** in the web UI. Unlike typical Gantt implementations that pull in heavy charting libraries like D3 or Chart.js, this implementation is **pure React + CSS positioning** — zero additional dependencies.\n\n**Files changed:** 25 files, +635 lines, -20 lines  \n**Commit:** `17019fe` — \"add planned start and end dates, added gantt view\"\n\n---\n\n### 3.2 Data Model Extension\n\n**New fields added to core types (`src/types/index.ts`):**\n\n```typescript\nexport interface Task {\n  // ... existing fields\n  plannedStart?: string;  // \"YYYY-MM-DD\" or \"YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm\"\n  plannedEnd?: string;\n}\n\nexport interface TaskCreateInput {\n  // ...\n  plannedStart?: string;\n  plannedEnd?: string;\n}\n\nexport interface TaskUpdateInput {\n  // ...\n  plannedStart?: string;\n  plannedEnd?: string;\n}\n```\n\n**`TaskEditArgs` and `task-edit-builder.ts`** also extended so CLI `--planned-start` / `--planned-end` options flow through the entire edit pipeline.\n\n---\n\n### 3.3 Markdown Persistence\n\nDates are stored in task frontmatter using snake_case keys (`planned_start`, `planned_end`) and normalized through the existing `normalizeDate()` helper:\n\n**Parser (`src/markdown/parser.ts`):**\n```typescript\nplannedStart: frontmatter.planned_start ? normalizeDate(frontmatter.planned_start) : undefined,\nplannedEnd: frontmatter.planned_end ? normalizeDate(frontmatter.planned_end) : undefined,\n```\n\n**Serializer (`src/markdown/serializer.ts`):**\n```typescript\n...(task.plannedStart && { planned_start: task.plannedStart }),\n...(task.plannedEnd && { planned_end: task.plannedEnd }),\n```\n\n**Test coverage:** `src/test/markdown.test.ts` — verifies parse + round-trip of planned dates with both date-only and datetime formats.\n\n---\n\n### 3.4 CLI Integration\n\nNew options added to `backlog task create` and `backlog task edit`:\n\n```bash\nbacklog task create \"My task\" --planned-start 2025-12-01 --planned-end 2025-12-15\nbacklog task edit TASK-123 --planned-start 2025-12-01\n```\n\n**Implementation (`src/cli.ts`):**\n```typescript\n.option(\"--planned-start <date>\", \"set planned start date (YYYY-MM-DD)\")\n.option(\"--planned-end <date>\", \"set planned end date (YYYY-MM-DD)\")\n```\n\nThe values flow through `TaskEditArgs` → `buildTaskUpdateInput()` → `Core.updateTask()` → markdown serializer.\n\n---\n\n### 3.5 Server API & MCP\n\n**Server (`src/server/index.ts`):**\n- Create endpoint passes `plannedStart` / `plannedEnd` into `Core.createTask()`\n- Update endpoint checks for string-typed `plannedStart` / `plannedEnd` in the request payload\n\n**MCP (`src/mcp/tools/tasks/handlers.ts` + `src/mcp/utils/schema-generators.ts`):**\n- `TaskCreateArgs` and `TaskListArgs` extended with optional date fields\n- JSON Schema descriptions: `\"Optional planned start date (YYYY-MM-DD or YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm)\"`\n- Max length validation: 25 characters\n\n---\n\n### 3.6 Web UI — Task Details Modal\n\n**New section in `TaskDetailsModal.tsx`** — two `type=\"date\"` inputs under a \"Planned Dates\" collapsible section:\n\n```tsx\n<input\n  type=\"date\"\n  value={plannedStart}\n  onChange={(e) => {\n    const value = e.target.value;\n    setPlannedStart(value);\n    void handleInlinePlannedDateChange(\"plannedStart\", value);\n  }}\n/>\n```\n\n**Inline update pattern:** Changes are saved immediately via `apiClient.updateTask()` (optimistic local state + websocket-driven background refresh). The modal does not close or trigger a full page reload — the global `tasks` array updates through the existing websocket `tasks-updated` event.\n\n**Dirty-checking:** `plannedStart` and `plannedEnd` are included in the `isDirty` memo so the \"Save\" button state and \"discard changes?\" prompt work correctly.\n\n---\n\n### 3.7 Web UI — GanttPage (核心实现)\n\n**File:** `src/web/components/GanttPage.tsx` (316 lines)  \n**Approach:** Zero external charting libraries. Pure React + Tailwind CSS + percentage-based positioning.\n\n#### Architecture\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  GanttPage                                                  │\n│  ├── useMemo: ganttTasks  (filter tasks with dates)        │\n│  ├── useMemo: fullRange   (min/max across all tasks)       │\n│  ├── useMemo: range       (viewport based on zoom)         │\n│  ├── useMemo: ticks       (time axis labels)               │\n│  └── Render:                                               │\n│       ├── Zoom control bar                                 │\n│       ├── Time axis (proportional tick labels)             │\n│       └── Task rows (title column + bar column)            │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n#### 1. Task filtering & normalization\n\n```typescript\nconst ganttTasks: GanttTask[] = useMemo(() => {\n  return tasks\n    .map((task) => {\n      const start = task.plannedStart ? toDate(task.plannedStart) : null;\n      const end = task.plannedEnd ? toDate(task.plannedEnd) : null;\n      if (!start && !end) return null;  // skip tasks without dates\n      const normalizedStart = start ?? end;\n      const normalizedEnd = end ?? start;\n      // ...\n    })\n    .filter((t): t is GanttTask => Boolean(t));\n}, [tasks]);\n```\n\n**Graceful handling:** Tasks without `plannedStart`/`plannedEnd` are silently excluded. An empty state message explains how to add dates.\n\n#### 2. Date parsing\n\n```typescript\nfunction toDate(value: string): Date | null {\n  if (!value) return null;\n  const hasTime = value.includes(\" \") || value.includes(\"T\");\n  const iso = value.replace(\" \", \"T\") + (hasTime ? \":00Z\" : \"T00:00:00Z\");\n  const d = new Date(iso);\n  return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;\n}\n```\n\nSupports both `YYYY-MM-DD` and `YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm` formats. Normalizes to UTC ISO strings internally.\n\n#### 3. Zoom levels & viewport\n\n```typescript\ntype ZoomLevel = \"1d\" | \"7d\" | \"30d\" | \"90d\" | \"365d\" | \"all\";\n```\n\n| Zoom | Behaviour |\n|------|-----------|\n| `all` | Shows full range from earliest task start to latest task end |\n| `1d` / `7d` / `30d` / `90d` / `365d` | Anchors to \"today\", shows window backward/forward from current date |\n\n**Viewport calculation:**\n```typescript\nconst now = Date.now();\nconst start = now - dayMs; // 1 day before today\nconst min = start;\nconst max = min + windowMs;\n```\n\n#### 4. Smart time axis ticks\n\nTick density adapts to zoom level:\n\n| Zoom | Tick step | Alignment |\n|------|-----------|-----------|\n| `1d`, `7d` | Daily | Midnight |\n| `30d` | Weekly | Monday-aligned |\n| `90d`, `365d`, `all` | Monthly | 1st of month |\n\n```typescript\n// 30d window: align to Monday\nconst diffToMonday = (day + 6) % 7;\nstartDate.setDate(startDate.getDate() - diffToMonday);\n```\n\n#### 5. Bar positioning (核心渲染逻辑)\n\n**No SVG, no canvas — pure CSS:**\n\n```tsx\n<div className=\"flex-1 relative h-6\">\n  {/* Background grid line */}\n  <div className=\"absolute inset-y-2 left-0 right-0 border-t border-dashed\" />\n  \n  {/* The bar */}\n  <div\n    className={`absolute top-0 bottom-0 rounded-full ${color} shadow-sm`}\n    style={{\n      left: `${startOffset}%`,   // percentage from viewport left\n      width: `${width}%`,        // percentage of viewport span\n    }}\n    title={`${formatDay(task.plannedStart)} → ${formatDay(task.plannedEnd)}`}\n  />\n</div>\n```\n\n**Position math:**\n```typescript\nconst startOffset = ((clampedStart - range.min) / range.span) * 100;\nconst width = Math.max(((clampedEnd - clampedStart) / range.span) * 100, 2);\n// Minimum 2% width so short tasks remain visible\n```\n\n**Clamping:** Tasks extending beyond the viewport are clipped:\n```typescript\nconst clampedStart = Math.max(startMs, range.min);\nconst clampedEnd = Math.min(endMs, range.max);\nif (clampedEnd <= range.min || clampedStart >= range.max) return null;\n```\n\n#### 6. Status colors\n\n```typescript\nconst color =\n  status.includes(\"done\") || status.includes(\"complete\")\n    ? \"bg-green-500\"\n    : status.includes(\"progress\")\n      ? \"bg-blue-500\"\n      : \"bg-gray-400\";\n```\n\n- **Green** = Done / Complete\n- **Blue** = In Progress\n- **Gray** = Default (To Do, etc.)\n\n#### 7. Interactivity\n\n- **Click a task row** → opens `TaskDetailsModal` via `onEditTask` callback\n- **Hover over bar** → native browser tooltip shows date range\n- **Zoom buttons** → instant re-render, no network request\n\n---\n\n### 3.8 Navigation & Routing\n\n**SideNavigation (`src/web/components/SideNavigation.tsx`):**\n- New \"Gantt\" nav item with custom SVG icon (horizontal bar chart shape)\n- Added to both expanded sidebar and collapsed icon-only mode\n\n**App.tsx routing:**\n```tsx\n<Route\n  path=\"gantt\"\n  element={<GanttPage tasks={tasks} onEditTask={handleEditTask} />}\n/>\n```\n\nThe Gantt page receives the full `tasks` array from App's state (already loaded via websocket), so no additional API call is needed on navigation.\n\n---\n\n### 3.9 Technical Highlights\n\n| Decision | Rationale |\n|----------|-----------|\n| **No charting library** | Keeps bundle small; Tailwind CSS handles all styling; easier to maintain |\n| **Percentage positioning** | Responsive without ResizeObserver; reflows naturally with container width |\n| **Date strings (not Date objects)** in Task type | Consistent with existing `createdDate` / `updatedDate` pattern; easy markdown round-trip |\n| **UTC normalization internally** | Avoids timezone bugs; display uses `toLocaleDateString()` for user-local formatting |\n| **Inline date update** | Immediate feedback; websocket syncs across clients automatically |\n| **2% minimum bar width** | Prevents single-day tasks from disappearing at wide zoom levels |\n| **Monday-aligned weekly ticks** | Follows ISO week convention; familiar to project planners |\n\n---\n\n### 3.10 Comparison with Typical Gantt Implementations\n\n| Aspect | Typical (D3/Chart.js) | andrewlongman07 approach |\n|--------|----------------------|--------------------------|\n| Dependencies | +1 heavy library | Zero new dependencies |\n| Bundle size | +50-200 KB | ~0 KB overhead |\n| Rendering | SVG/Canvas | DOM divs + CSS |\n| Responsiveness | Requires ResizeObserver | Natural CSS reflow |\n| Customization | Library-specific APIs | Standard React/JSX/Tailwind |\n| Drag-resize bars | Complex event handling | Not implemented (click-to-edit) |\n| Dependencies between tasks | Arrow drawing | Not implemented |\n\n**Trade-off:** Simpler and lighter, but lacks advanced features like drag-resize, dependency arrows, or critical path analysis. For a lightweight task tracker, this is a pragmatic balance.\n\n---\n\n### 3.11 Potential Upstream Value\n\nThis implementation is **highly upstreamable** because:\n\n1. **No breaking changes** — `plannedStart`/`plannedEnd` are optional fields everywhere\n2. **Follows existing patterns** — frontmatter serialization, type extensions, and CLI option patterns match the codebase conventions\n3. **Self-contained** — GanttPage is a single component file; easy to review and merge\n4. **Tested** — Markdown parse/serialize tests included\n5. **MCP-ready** — AI agents can already read/write planned dates through MCP tools\n\n**Suggested upstream improvements:**\n- Add date validation (reject end < start at API level)\n- Add overdue highlighting (red tint when `plannedEnd < today && status !== done`)\n- Add drag-to-resize bars (would require mouse event handling + optimistic API updates)\n- Export Gantt view as PNG/SVG for reporting\n",
  "mode": "append"
}

已写完，报告里新增了一个完整的 **Deep Dive: andrewlongman07 — Gantt Timeline & Scheduling** 章节。

### 技术路线核心要点

**1. 零依赖的纯 React/CSS 实现**
- 没有用 D3、Chart.js 或任何图表库
- 甘特条用普通的 `<div>` + `absolute` 定位 + `left`/`width` 百分比实现
- 好处：bundle 零增重、完全响应式、用标准 Tailwind 就能改样式

**2. 数据流设计**
```
Frontmatter (planned_start/planned_end)
    ↓  parser.ts normalizeDate()
Task 类型里的 string 字段
    ↓  serializer.ts 回写 frontmatter
CLI (--planned-start/--planned-end)
    ↓  Core.createTask / Core.updateTask
Server API / MCP / Web UI 全部打通
```

**3. GanttPage 渲染逻辑**
- **任务过滤**：只显示有 `plannedStart` 或 `plannedEnd` 的任务，没有日期的自动排除并显示空状态提示
- **视口计算**：6 级缩放（1d/7d/30d/90d/365d/all），`all` 显示全部任务范围，其他以「今天」为中心锚点
- **时间轴刻度**：根据缩放级别智能切换——短窗口按天、30 天按周（周一对齐）、长窗口按月（1 号对齐）
- **条形定位**：
  ```css
  left: ${((start - range.min) / range.span) * 100}%
  width: ${max((duration / range.span) * 100, 2)}%
  ```
  最短 2% 宽度保证单日任务不会消失
- **状态颜色**：绿色=完成、蓝色=进行中、灰色=默认

**4. 交互设计**
- 点击任务行 → 打开 TaskDetailsModal 编辑
- 日期输入框用 `type="date"`，变更即时保存（optimistic update + websocket 后台同步）
- 悬停条显示日期范围 tooltip

**5. 可 upstream 性很高**
- 无破坏性变更，字段全可选
- 遵循现有代码风格（frontmatter、类型扩展、CLI 选项模式）
- 单文件组件，易于 review

1. **High Retention Rate**: 50.3% of forks have active pushes within the last 6 months, indicating strong user engagement rather than "fork-and-forget" behavior.

2. **Recent Surge**: A significant wave of forks occurred in early-to-mid May 2026, with the most recent active contributions happening within the last week.

4. **Sustained Interest**: Forks created as early as July 2025 continue to receive updates as of May 2026, showing long-term adoption.

## Deep Dive: Two Notable Forks

> The following section details the exact changes made by **SkogBackup/backlog** and **MensNetwork/backlog.md**, based on direct repository analysis (commit diffs, file structures, and source code).

---

### 1. SkogBackup/backlog — "skogbacklog" Rebrand & Clean Slate

**What they did:**
Took the Backlog.md template and performed a full rebrand plus aggressive data cleanup, turning it into a blank slate for their own use.

**How they did it:**

#### 1.1 Brand Rename (`backlog/config.yml`)

| Field | Before | After |
|-------|--------|-------|
| `project_name` | `"Backlog.md"` | `"skogbacklog"` |
| `statuses` | `["To Do", "In Progress", "Done"]` | `["todo", "doing", "done"]` |
| `default_editor` | `"rider"` | `"nvim"` |
| `default_port` | `6420` | `3000` |
| `auto_commit` | `false` | `true` |
| `zero_padded_ids` | `0` | `3` |
| `active_branch_days` | `10` | `90` |
| `task_prefix` | `"back"` | `"skog"` |

#### 1.2 Mass Deletion of Template Data

All backlog content from the upstream template was wiped in 6 sequential commits:

| Commit | Action | Files Deleted | Lines Removed |
|--------|--------|---------------|---------------|
| `04efcc3` | clean tasks | 137 task files | 8,573 |
| `6917b45` | clean archive | 44 archive files | 1,162 |
| `ea78396` | clean completed | 303 completed tasks | 17,501 |
| `ab738f7` | clean drafts | 16 draft files | 425 |
| `0fefc81` | clean milestones | 1 milestone | 8 |
| `05afd21` | clean decisions | 1 decision doc | 100 |

#### 1.3 Resulting Structure

After cleanup, only 12 files remain in `backlog/`:

```
backlog/
├── archive/drafts/readme.md
├── archive/readme.md
├── archive/tasks/readme.md
├── assets/images/web.jpeg
├── config.yml
├── decisions/readme.md
├── docs/doc-001 - Testing-Style-Guide.md
├── docs/doc-002 - Configuring-VIM-and-Neovim-as-a-Default-Editor.md
├── docs/readme.md
├── milestones/readme.md
├── readme.md
└── tasks/readme.md
```

**Key insight:** This is essentially a "factory reset" — they kept the tool and its documentation, but stripped every bit of demo/example data to start fresh with their own `skog`-prefixed tasks.

---

### 2. MensNetwork/backlog.md — Inbox System, Dept Labels & Subtask Hierarchy

**What they did:**
Built significant product-level features on top of Backlog.md:
1. A full **Inbox/Messaging** system with department-level routing
2. **Department label colours** on kanban board and filter bar
3. **Subtask hierarchy** navigation in task detail view
4. **Board filter enhancements** (active-only, priority sort, OR label filter)
5. **Production deployment docs** for ARM64 cross-compile

**How they did it:**

---

#### 2.1 Inbox / Messaging System (TASK-101)

A complete two-panel messaging UI ( Gmail-style ) added to the Web UI.

**Server-side API (3 new endpoints in `src/server/index.ts`):**

| Endpoint | Method | Purpose |
|----------|--------|---------|
| `/api/inbox` | GET | Scan `departments/{dept}/inbox` and `modules/{module}/inbox` dirs, return message list |
| `/api/inbox/message?path=` | GET | Read a single message's markdown content (with path traversal security check) |
| `/api/inbox/archive` | POST | Move a message file to its `processed/` subdirectory |

**File system convention assumed:**

```
{c-suite-root}/
├── departments/
│   ├── ceo/inbox/
│   ├── cto/inbox/
│   ├── cmo/inbox/
│   └── cfo/inbox/
└── modules/
    └── {module-name}/inbox/
```

Messages are Markdown files with YAML frontmatter:
```yaml
---
title: "Subject line"
from: "sender"
to: "recipient"
type: "memo"
priority: "high"
date: "2026-04-07"
---
Body content here...
```

**Client-side (`src/web/components/InboxPage.tsx`, 501 lines):**

- **Left panel**: scrollable message list with
  - Unread blue dot indicator
  - Department badge (CEO/CTO/CMO/CFO) with colour coding
  - Module badge for module messages
  - Date + sender + preview
  - Selection highlight
- **Right panel**: message detail view with
  - Title, metadata row (from/to/priority/date)
  - Department badge
  - **Mark read/unread** toggle button
  - **Archive** button (moves file to `processed/`)
  - "Archived" status badge for processed messages
  - Body rendered via `MermaidMarkdown` component
- **Toolbar filters**:
  - Department tabs: All | CEO | CTO | CMO | CFO
  - Module pills (dynamically discovered)
  - "Show archived" checkbox
  - Unread count badge
- **Read state**: persisted in `localStorage` (`backlog:inbox:read`); auto-marks as read after 5 seconds of selection

**SideNavigation integration (`src/web/components/SideNavigation.tsx`):**

- New "Inbox" nav item with envelope icon
- Unread count badge fetched from `/api/inbox` on mount and when leaving inbox page
- Badge shows in both expanded sidebar (`ml-auto` pill) and collapsed sidebar (top-right dot, "9+" cap)

---

#### 2.2 Department Label Colours (TASK-101)

**New file: `src/web/utils/label-colours.ts`**

Maps `dept:*` labels to a predefined colour scheme:

| Label | Background | Text | Border |
|-------|-----------|------|--------|
| `dept:ceo` | `#1e2d4a` | `#60a5fa` | `#60a5fa33` |
| `dept:cto` | `#1e3a2f` | `#4ade80` | `#4ade8033` |
| `dept:cmo` | `#2d1e4a` | `#c084fc` | `#c084fc33` |
| `dept:cfo` | `#3a2a1e` | `#fb923c` | `#fb923c33` |

**Applied in two places:**

1. **`TaskCard.tsx`** — labels starting with `dept:` are displayed as uppercase badges with the matching colour:
   ```tsx
   const colour = getDeptLabelColour(label);
   const display = label.startsWith("dept:") ? label.slice(5).toUpperCase() : label;
   ```

2. **`Board.tsx` label filter bar** — label buttons use the same colour scheme when active/inactive:
   ```tsx
   const style = colour
     ? active
       ? { backgroundColor: colour.activeBg, color: colour.activeText, ... }
       : { backgroundColor: colour.bg, color: colour.text, ... }
     : undefined;
   ```

---

  ```tsx

    <button onClick={() => handleTaskNavigation(task.parentTaskId)}>
      <span>{task.parentTaskId}</span>

**Navigation logic:**

- **`src/web/utils/navigate-to-task.ts`** — extracted helper that:
  1. Calls `apiClient.fetchTask(taskId)` via API
  2. On failure, falls back to local `tasks` array
  3. On total failure, sets a navigation error (auto-clears after 4s)
  4. Manages `isNavigating` state + `isFreshFetch` flag

- **`App.tsx`** — `handleNavigateToTask` callback wired to `TaskDetailsModal`'s `onNavigateToTask` prop

- **`TaskDetailsModal.tsx`** — `handleTaskNavigation` wrapper that prompts "Discard unsaved changes?" if in edit/create mode

**Board / Kanban visual hierarchy:**

- **`Board.tsx`**: `filteredTasks` explicitly excludes subtasks from the board view:
  ```tsx
  let result = tasks.filter(task => !task.parentTaskId);
  ```

- **`TaskColumn.tsx`**: Subtasks are reordered to appear directly beneath their parent within the same column, with visual indentation:
  ```tsx
  {orderedTasks.map(({ task, isSubtask }) => (
    <div className={`relative ${isSubtask ? "ml-4 pl-2 border-l-2 border-gray-200" : ""}`}>
      <TaskCard task={task} ... />
    </div>
  ))}
  ```

**Tests:** `src/web/__tests__/subtask-navigation.test.ts` — 8 test cases covering API success, local fallback, error handling, and rendering conditions.

---

#### 2.4 Board Filter Enhancements (BACK-42)

**Active-only toggle:**

- Filters tasks and hides matching status columns when enabled

**Priority sort:**

- Click to toggle inclusion; multiple selections use OR logic
- `dept:*` labels render with department colour scheme

**Milestone lane improvements:**
- Empty lanes hidden in milestone mode (`visibleLanes` memo)
- Lane headers only shown when 2+ visible lanes exist
- When filtering by milestone, non-matching lanes auto-collapsed

## 3. Deep Dive: andrewlongman07 — Gantt Timeline & Scheduling

**Fork:** [andrewlongman07/Backlog.md](https://github.com/andrewlongman07/Backlog.md)  
**Created:** 2025-12-08 | **Last pushed:** 2025-12-17 | **Gap:** 9 days  
**Key feature:** Planned start/end dates + browser Gantt chart view

### 3.1 Overview

This fork adds **project planning capabilities** to Backlog.md through two new task fields (`plannedStart` / `plannedEnd`) and a fully client-side **Gantt timeline view** in the web UI. Unlike typical Gantt implementations that pull in heavy charting libraries like D3 or Chart.js, this implementation is **pure React + CSS positioning** — zero additional dependencies.

**Files changed:** 25 files, +635 lines, -20 lines  
**Commit:** `17019fe` — "add planned start and end dates, added gantt view"

---

### 3.2 Data Model Extension

**New fields added to core types (`src/types/index.ts`):**

```typescript
export interface Task {
  // ... existing fields
  plannedStart?: string;  // "YYYY-MM-DD" or "YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm"
  plannedEnd?: string;
}

export interface TaskCreateInput {
  // ...
  plannedStart?: string;
  plannedEnd?: string;
}

export interface TaskUpdateInput {
  // ...
  plannedStart?: string;
  plannedEnd?: string;
}
```

**`TaskEditArgs` and `task-edit-builder.ts`** also extended so CLI `--planned-start` / `--planned-end` options flow through the entire edit pipeline.

---

### 3.3 Markdown Persistence

Dates are stored in task frontmatter using snake_case keys (`planned_start`, `planned_end`) and normalized through the existing `normalizeDate()` helper:

**Parser (`src/markdown/parser.ts`):**
```typescript
plannedStart: frontmatter.planned_start ? normalizeDate(frontmatter.planned_start) : undefined,
plannedEnd: frontmatter.planned_end ? normalizeDate(frontmatter.planned_end) : undefined,
```

**Serializer (`src/markdown/serializer.ts`):**
```typescript
...(task.plannedStart && { planned_start: task.plannedStart }),
...(task.plannedEnd && { planned_end: task.plannedEnd }),
```

**Test coverage:** `src/test/markdown.test.ts` — verifies parse + round-trip of planned dates with both date-only and datetime formats.

---

### 3.4 CLI Integration

New options added to `backlog task create` and `backlog task edit`:

```bash
backlog task create "My task" --planned-start 2025-12-01 --planned-end 2025-12-15
backlog task edit TASK-123 --planned-start 2025-12-01
```

**Implementation (`src/cli.ts`):**
```typescript
.option("--planned-start <date>", "set planned start date (YYYY-MM-DD)")
.option("--planned-end <date>", "set planned end date (YYYY-MM-DD)")
```

The values flow through `TaskEditArgs` → `buildTaskUpdateInput()` → `Core.updateTask()` → markdown serializer.

---

### 3.5 Server API & MCP

**Server (`src/server/index.ts`):**
- Create endpoint passes `plannedStart` / `plannedEnd` into `Core.createTask()`
- Update endpoint checks for string-typed `plannedStart` / `plannedEnd` in the request payload

**MCP (`src/mcp/tools/tasks/handlers.ts` + `src/mcp/utils/schema-generators.ts`):**
- `TaskCreateArgs` and `TaskListArgs` extended with optional date fields
- JSON Schema descriptions: `"Optional planned start date (YYYY-MM-DD or YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm)"`
- Max length validation: 25 characters

---

### 3.6 Web UI — Task Details Modal

**New section in `TaskDetailsModal.tsx`** — two `type="date"` inputs under a "Planned Dates" collapsible section:

```tsx
<input
  type="date"
  value={plannedStart}
  onChange={(e) => {
    const value = e.target.value;
    setPlannedStart(value);
    void handleInlinePlannedDateChange("plannedStart", value);
  }}
/>
```

**Inline update pattern:** Changes are saved immediately via `apiClient.updateTask()` (optimistic local state + websocket-driven background refresh). The modal does not close or trigger a full page reload — the global `tasks` array updates through the existing websocket `tasks-updated` event.

**Dirty-checking:** `plannedStart` and `plannedEnd` are included in the `isDirty` memo so the "Save" button state and "discard changes?" prompt work correctly.

---

### 3.7 Web UI — GanttPage (核心实现)

**File:** `src/web/components/GanttPage.tsx` (316 lines)  
**Approach:** Zero external charting libraries. Pure React + Tailwind CSS + percentage-based positioning.

#### Architecture

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  GanttPage                                                  │
│  ├── useMemo: ganttTasks  (filter tasks with dates)        │
│  ├── useMemo: fullRange   (min/max across all tasks)       │
│  ├── useMemo: range       (viewport based on zoom)         │
│  ├── useMemo: ticks       (time axis labels)               │
│  └── Render:                                               │
│       ├── Zoom control bar                                 │
│       ├── Time axis (proportional tick labels)             │
│       └── Task rows (title column + bar column)            │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

#### 1. Task filtering & normalization

```typescript
const ganttTasks: GanttTask[] = useMemo(() => {
  return tasks
    .map((task) => {
      const start = task.plannedStart ? toDate(task.plannedStart) : null;
      const end = task.plannedEnd ? toDate(task.plannedEnd) : null;
      if (!start && !end) return null;  // skip tasks without dates
      const normalizedStart = start ?? end;
      const normalizedEnd = end ?? start;
      // ...
    })
    .filter((t): t is GanttTask => Boolean(t));
}, [tasks]);
```

**Graceful handling:** Tasks without `plannedStart`/`plannedEnd` are silently excluded. An empty state message explains how to add dates.

#### 2. Date parsing

```typescript
function toDate(value: string): Date | null {
  if (!value) return null;
  const hasTime = value.includes(" ") || value.includes("T");
  const iso = value.replace(" ", "T") + (hasTime ? ":00Z" : "T00:00:00Z");
  const d = new Date(iso);
  return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;
}
```

Supports both `YYYY-MM-DD` and `YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm` formats. Normalizes to UTC ISO strings internally.

#### 3. Zoom levels & viewport

```typescript
type ZoomLevel = "1d" | "7d" | "30d" | "90d" | "365d" | "all";
```

| Zoom | Behaviour |
|------|-----------|
| `all` | Shows full range from earliest task start to latest task end |
| `1d` / `7d` / `30d` / `90d` / `365d` | Anchors to "today", shows window backward/forward from current date |

**Viewport calculation:**
```typescript
const now = Date.now();
const start = now - dayMs; // 1 day before today
const min = start;
const max = min + windowMs;
```

#### 4. Smart time axis ticks

Tick density adapts to zoom level:

| Zoom | Tick step | Alignment |
|------|-----------|-----------|
| `1d`, `7d` | Daily | Midnight |
| `30d` | Weekly | Monday-aligned |
| `90d`, `365d`, `all` | Monthly | 1st of month |

```typescript
// 30d window: align to Monday
const diffToMonday = (day + 6) % 7;
startDate.setDate(startDate.getDate() - diffToMonday);
```

#### 5. Bar positioning (核心渲染逻辑)

**No SVG, no canvas — pure CSS:**

```tsx
<div className="flex-1 relative h-6">
  {/* Background grid line */}
  <div className="absolute inset-y-2 left-0 right-0 border-t border-dashed" />
  
  {/* The bar */}
  <div
    className={`absolute top-0 bottom-0 rounded-full ${color} shadow-sm`}
    style={{
      left: `${startOffset}%`,   // percentage from viewport left
      width: `${width}%`,        // percentage of viewport span
    }}
    title={`${formatDay(task.plannedStart)} → ${formatDay(task.plannedEnd)}`}
  />
</div>
```

**Position math:**
```typescript
const startOffset = ((clampedStart - range.min) / range.span) * 100;
const width = Math.max(((clampedEnd - clampedStart) / range.span) * 100, 2);
// Minimum 2% width so short tasks remain visible
```

**Clamping:** Tasks extending beyond the viewport are clipped:
```typescript
const clampedStart = Math.max(startMs, range.min);
const clampedEnd = Math.min(endMs, range.max);
if (clampedEnd <= range.min || clampedStart >= range.max) return null;
```

#### 6. Status colors

```typescript
const color =
  status.includes("done") || status.includes("complete")
    ? "bg-green-500"
    : status.includes("progress")
      ? "bg-blue-500"
      : "bg-gray-400";
```

- **Green** = Done / Complete
- **Blue** = In Progress
- **Gray** = Default (To Do, etc.)

#### 7. Interactivity

- **Click a task row** → opens `TaskDetailsModal` via `onEditTask` callback
- **Hover over bar** → native browser tooltip shows date range
- **Zoom buttons** → instant re-render, no network request

---

### 3.8 Navigation & Routing

**SideNavigation (`src/web/components/SideNavigation.tsx`):**
- New "Gantt" nav item with custom SVG icon (horizontal bar chart shape)
- Added to both expanded sidebar and collapsed icon-only mode

**App.tsx routing:**
```tsx
<Route
  path="gantt"
  element={<GanttPage tasks={tasks} onEditTask={handleEditTask} />}
/>
```

The Gantt page receives the full `tasks` array from App's state (already loaded via websocket), so no additional API call is needed on navigation.

---

### 3.9 Technical Highlights

| Decision | Rationale |
|----------|-----------|
| **No charting library** | Keeps bundle small; Tailwind CSS handles all styling; easier to maintain |
| **Percentage positioning** | Responsive without ResizeObserver; reflows naturally with container width |
| **Date strings (not Date objects)** in Task type | Consistent with existing `createdDate` / `updatedDate` pattern; easy markdown round-trip |
| **UTC normalization internally** | Avoids timezone bugs; display uses `toLocaleDateString()` for user-local formatting |
| **Inline date update** | Immediate feedback; websocket syncs across clients automatically |
| **2% minimum bar width** | Prevents single-day tasks from disappearing at wide zoom levels |
| **Monday-aligned weekly ticks** | Follows ISO week convention; familiar to project planners |

---

### 3.10 Comparison with Typical Gantt Implementations

| Aspect | Typical (D3/Chart.js) | andrewlongman07 approach |
|--------|----------------------|--------------------------|
| Dependencies | +1 heavy library | Zero new dependencies |
| Bundle size | +50-200 KB | ~0 KB overhead |
| Rendering | SVG/Canvas | DOM divs + CSS |
| Responsiveness | Requires ResizeObserver | Natural CSS reflow |
| Customization | Library-specific APIs | Standard React/JSX/Tailwind |
| Drag-resize bars | Complex event handling | Not implemented (click-to-edit) |
| Dependencies between tasks | Arrow drawing | Not implemented |

**Trade-off:** Simpler and lighter, but lacks advanced features like drag-resize, dependency arrows, or critical path analysis. For a lightweight task tracker, this is a pragmatic balance.

---

### 3.11 Potential Upstream Value

This implementation is **highly upstreamable** because:

1. **No breaking changes** — `plannedStart`/`plannedEnd` are optional fields everywhere
2. **Follows existing patterns** — frontmatter serialization, type extensions, and CLI option patterns match the codebase conventions
3. **Self-contained** — GanttPage is a single component file; easy to review and merge
4. **Tested** — Markdown parse/serialize tests included
5. **MCP-ready** — AI agents can already read/write planned dates through MCP tools

**Suggested upstream improvements:**
- Add date validation (reject end < start at API level)
- Add overdue highlighting (red tint when `plannedEnd < today && status !== done`)
- Add drag-to-resize bars (would require mouse event handling + optimistic API updates)
- Export Gantt view as PNG/SVG for reporting


</details>

OpenSpecBacklog.md | normful |\n| Backlog.md | fork-Backlog.md | woss |\n| Backlog.md | agentMap.md | garyqi-to |\n| Backlog.md | my-backlog.md | ferreirase |\n| Backlog.md | study-Backlog.md | cpu-once |\n| Backlog.md | backlog | SkogBackup |\n| Backlog.md | kaseruka | wagleanuj |\n\n## 关键洞察\n\n1. **高留存率**：50.3% 的 Fork 在近 6 个月内有活跃推送，表明用户参与度很高，而非「Fork 后就遗忘」的行为。\n\n2. **近期激增**：2026 年 5 月初至中旬出现了一波显著的 Fork 浪潮，最新的活跃贡献发生在过去一周内。\n\n3. **自定义趋势**：多个用户重命名了他们的 Fork，表明他们正在针对特定用例调整项目（例如 OpenSpec 集成、个人待办系统、学习工具）。\n\n4. **持续兴趣**：早在 2025 年 7 月创建的 Fork 仍在 2026 年 5 月接收更新，显示出长期采用。\n\n---\n\n## 提交分析（过去一年）\n\n> 分析过去一年的提交信息（自 2025-05-23 起），排除错误修复、格式化、代码检查和拼写更正。重点关注功能改进和架构变更。\n\n### 图例\n\n| 标签 | 含义 |\n|-----|---------|\n| 🔄 | 主要为上游同步；未识别出独特的功能变更 |\n| 🏗️ | 显著的架构或功能修改 |\n| 📦 | 针对特定用例的自定义/重品牌 |\n\n### 重命名/自定义 Fork\n\n| 仓库 | 标签 | 功能摘要 |\n|-----------|-----|-------------------|\n| **normful/OpenSpecBacklog.md** | 🏗️ | 将 DAG 引擎替换为扁平变更检查清单；移除了 zod + yaml 依赖，改用纯 TypeScript 验证器；在 frontmatter 中添加了本地时区偏移 ISO 8601 时间戳；创建了 backlog 目录结构文档 |\n| **woss/fork-Backlog.md** | 🔄 | 主要为上游同步 |\n| **garyqi-to/agentMap.md** | 📦🏗️ | 重品牌为 agent-native 项目，带有自定义路线图结构；为任务列表添加了里程碑过滤支持（CLI 和 MCP）；重构了 TUI 任务列表和看板之间的过滤 UX 一致性 |\n| **ferreirase/my-backlog.md** | 🔄 | 主要为上游同步 |\n| **cpu-once/study-Backlog.md** | 🏗️ | 为任务添加了 touched-files 字段，支持基于文件名的搜索；为 Backlog 二进制和 MCP 原型化了 Codex 插件；添加了 Cursor 作为原生第一方代理选项 **manuel71sj/Backlog.md** | 🔄 | 主要为上游同步 |\n| **n16h7h4wk00/Backlog.md** | 🔄 | 主要为上游同步 |\n| **rekrevs/Backlog.md** | 🔄 | 主要为上游同步 |\n| **samvincent/Backlog.md** | 🔄 | 主要为上游同步 |\n\n---\n\n### 分析备注\n\n- 大多数活跃 Fork（约 70%）是**上游同步**——用户保持他们的 Fork 与原始仓库同步，没有添加独特的功能。\n- **显著的自定义**集中在重命名的 Fork 和一些活跃的维护者中：\n  - `normful/OpenSpecBacklog.md` — 架构重构（扁平检查清单引擎、纯 TS 验证器）\n  - `garyqi-to/agentMap.md` — 重品牌为 agent-native，UX 一致性改进\n  - `SkogBackup/backlog` — 完全重品牌和目录清理\n  - `wagleanuj/kaseruka` — 完全重品牌，自定义任务编号（TASK-xxx）\n  - `MensNetwork/backlog.md` — 大量 Web UI 添加（收件箱、部门标签、子任务层级）\n  - `cicero-im/Backlog.md` — 添加了 Mermaid 渲染和资源服务\n  - `lenucksi/Backlog.md` — 侧边栏部分、文件预览、CI 强制执行\n  - `kuwork/Backlog.md` — 带语法高亮的文件预览\n  - `cpu-once/study-Backlog.md` — Codex 插件原型和 Cursor 集成\n  - `x-logan23/Backlog.md` — Windows shell 支持和评审追踪文档\n- 大量的上游同步表明对核心项目的持续兴趣很强，即使用户尚未（或尚未）贡献独特的功能。\n\n---\n\n## 深度分析：两个值得注意的 Fork\n\n> 以下部分基于直接仓库分析（提交差异、文件结构和源代码），详细说明 **SkogBackup/backlog** 和 **MensNetwork/backlog.md** 所做的确切更改。\n\n---\n\n### 1. SkogBackup/backlog — \"skogbacklog\" 重品牌与清理\n\n**他们做了什么：**\n获取 Backlog.md 模板并执行了完全重品牌加激进的数据清理，将其转变为他们自己的空白画布。\n\n**他们是如何做的：**\n\n#### 1.1 品牌重命名 (`backlog/config.yml`)\n\n| 字段 | 修改前 | 修改后 |\n|-------|--------|-------|\n| `project_name` | `\"Backlog.md\"` | `\"skogbacklog\"` |\n| `statuses` | `[\"To Do\", \"In Progress\", \"Done\"]` | `[\"todo\", \"doing\", \"done\"]` |\n| `default_editor` | `\"rider\"` | `\"nvim\"` |\n| `default_port` | `6420` | `3000` |\n| `auto_commit` | `false` | `true` |\n| `zero_padded_ids` | `0` | `3` |\n| `active_branch_days` | `10` | `90` |\n| `task_prefix` | `\"back\"` | `\"skog\"` |\n\n#### 1.2 模板数据的大规模删除\n\n上游模板中的所有 backlog 内容在 6 个顺序提交中被清除：\n\n| 提交 | 操作 | 删除文件数 | 删除行数 |\n|--------|--------|---------------|---------------|\n| `04efcc3` | 清理任务 | 137 个任务文件 | 8,573 |\n| `6917b45` | 清理归档 | 44 个归档文件 | 1,162 |\n| `ea78396` | 清理已完成 | 303 个已完成任务 | 17,501 |\n| `ab738f7` | 清理草稿 | 16 个草稿文件 | 425 |\n| `0fefc81` | 清理里程碑 | 1 个里程碑 | 8 |\n| `05afd21` | 清理决策 | 1 个决策文档 | 100 |\n\n#### 1.3 结果结构\n\n清理后，`backlog/` 中仅保留 12 个文件：\n\n```\nbacklog/\n├── archive/drafts/readme.md\n├── archive/readme.md\n├── archive/tasks/readme.md\n├── assets/images/web.jpeg\n├── config.yml\n├── decisions/readme.md\n├── docs/doc-001 - Testing-Style-Guide.md\n├── docs/doc-002 - Configuring-VIM-and-Neovim-as-a-Default-Editor.md\n├── docs/readme.md\n├── milestones/readme.md\n├── readme.md\n└── tasks/readme.md\n```\n\n**关键洞察：** 这本质上是一次「恢复出厂设置」——他们保留了工具及其文档，但剥离了每一个演示/示例数据，以便用他们自己的 `skog` 前缀任务重新开始。\n\n---\n\n### 2. MensNetwork/backlog.md — 收件箱系统、部门标签和子任务层级\n\n**他们做了什么：**\n在 Backlog.md 之上构建了显著的产品级功能：\n1. 完整的**收件箱/消息**系统，带有部门级路由\n2. 看板看板和标签过滤栏上的**部门标签颜色**\n3. 任务详情视图中的**子任务层级**导航\n4. **看板过滤器增强**（仅活跃、优先级排序、OR 标签过滤）\n5. ARM64 交叉编译的**生产部署文档**\n\n**他们是如何做的：**\n\n---\n\n#### 2.1 收件箱/消息系统 (TASK-101)\n\n在 Web UI 中添加了一个完整的双面板消息 UI（Gmail 风格）。\n\n**服务器端 API（`src/server/index.ts` 中 3 个新端点）：**\n\n| 端点 | 方法 | 用途 |\n|----------|--------|--------|\n| `/api/inbox` | GET | 扫描 `departments/{dept}/inbox` 和 `modules/{module}/inbox` 目录，返回消息列表 |\n| `/api/inbox/message?path=` | GET | 读取单个消息的 markdown 内容（带有路径遍历安全检查） |\n| `/api/inbox/archive` | POST | 将消息文件移动到其 `processed/` 子目录 |\n\n**文件系统约定：**\n\n```\n{c-suite-root}/\n├── departments/\n│   ├── ceo/inbox/\n│   ├── cto/inbox/\n│   ├── cmo/inbox/\n│   └── cfo/inbox/\n└── modules/\n    └── {module-name}/inbox/\n```\n\n消息是带有 YAML frontmatter 的 Markdown 文件：\n```yaml\n---\ntitle: \"主题行\"\nfrom: \"发件人\"\nto: \"收件人\"\ntype: \"memo\"\npriority: \"high\"\ndate: \"2026-04-07\"\n---\n正文内容...\n```\n\n**客户端 (`src/web/components/InboxPage.tsx`，501 行)：**\n\n- **左侧面板**：可滚动的消息列表，带有\n  - 未读蓝色圆点指示器\n  - 部门徽章（CEO/CTO/CMO/CFO）带有颜色编码\n  - 模块消息模块徽章\n  - 日期 + 发件人 + 预览\n  - 选中高亮\n- **右侧面板**：消息详情视图，带有\n  - 标题、元数据行（发件人/收件人/优先级/日期）\n  - 部门徽章\n  - **标记已读/未读**切换按钮\n  - **归档**按钮（将文件移动到 `processed/`）\n  - 已处理消息的「已归档」状态徽章\n  - 通过 `MermaidMarkdown` 组件渲染正文\n- **工具栏过滤器**：\n  - 部门标签：全部 | CEO | CTO | CMO | CFO\n  - 模块标签（动态发现）\n  - 「显示已归档」复选框\n  - 未读计数徽章\n- **读取状态**：持久化在 `localStorage` (`backlog:inbox:read`) 中；选择后 5 秒自动标记为已读\n\n**SideNavigation 集成 (`src/web/components/SideNavigation.tsx`)：**\n\n- 新的「收件箱」导航项，带有信封图标\n- 未读计数徽章在挂载时和离开收件箱页面时从 `/api/inbox` 获取\n- 徽章在展开的侧边栏（`ml-auto` 标签）和折叠的侧边栏（右上角圆点，上限 \"9+\"）中显示\n\n---\n\n#### 2.2 部门标签颜色 (TASK-101)\n\n**新文件：`src/web/utils/label-colours.ts`**\n\n将 `dept:*` 标签映射到预定义的颜色方案：\n\n| 标签 | 背景色 | 文字色 | 边框色 |\n|-------|-----------|------|--------|\n| `dept:ceo` | `#1e2d4a` | `#60a5fa` | `#60a5fa33` |\n| `dept:cto` | `#1e3a2f` | `#4ade80` | `#4ade8033` |\n| `dept:cmo` | `#2d1e4a` | `#c084fc` | `#c084fc33` |\n| `dept:cfo` | `#3a2a1e` | `#fb923c` | `#fb923c33` |\n\n**在两个地方应用：**\n\n1. **`TaskCard.tsx`** — 以 `dept:` 开头的标签显示为大写徽章，带有匹配的颜色：\n   ```tsx\n   const colour = getDeptLabelColour(label);\n   const display = label.startsWith(\"dept:\") ? label.slice(5).toUpperCase() : label;\n   ```\n\n2. **`Board.tsx` 标签过滤栏** — 标签按钮在激活/非激活时使用相同的颜色方案：\n   ```tsx\n   const style = colour\n     ? active\n       ? { backgroundColor: colour.activeBg, color: colour.activeText, ... }\n       : { backgroundColor: colour.bg, color: colour.text, ... }\n     : undefined;\n   ```\n\n---\n\n#### 2.3 子任务层级 (BACK-42 / TASK-42)\n\n利用了 `Task` 类型中已有的 `parentTaskId` / `subtaskSummaries` 字段（已通过服务器端的 `attachSubtaskSummaries()` 填充）。\n\n**TaskDetailsModal.tsx 添加：**\n\n- **父任务部分**（查看子任务时）：\n  ```tsx\n  {task?.parentTaskId && (\n    <button onClick={() => handleTaskNavigation(task.parentTaskId)}>\n      <span>{task.parentTaskId}</span>\n      {task.parentTaskTitle && <span>{task.parentTaskTitle}</span>}\n    </button>\n  )}\n  ```\n\n- **子任务部分**（查看父任务时）：\n  ```tsx\n  {task?.subtaskSummaries && task.subtaskSummaries.length > 0 && (\n    <ul>\n      {task.subtaskSummaries.map((subtask) => (\n        <li key={subtask.id}>\n          <button onClick={() => handleTaskNavigation(subtask.id)}>\n            {subtask.id} — {subtask.title}\n          </button>\n        </li>\n      ))}\n    </ul>\n  )}\n  ```\n\n**导航逻辑：**\n\n- **`src/web/utils/navigate-to-task.ts`** — 提取的辅助函数：\n  1. 通过 API 调用 `apiClient.fetchTask(taskId)`\n  2. 失败时回退到本地 `tasks` 数组\n  3. 完全失败时设置导航错误（4 秒后自动清除）\n  4. 管理 `isNavigating` 状态 + `isFreshFetch` 标志\n\n- **`App.tsx`** — `handleNavigateToTask` 回调连接到 `TaskDetailsModal` 的 `onNavigateToTask` prop\n\n- **`TaskDetailsModal.tsx`** — `handleTaskNavigation` 包装器，如果在编辑/创建模式下会提示「丢弃未保存的更改？」\n\n**看板/看板视觉层级：**\n\n- **`Board.tsx`**：`filteredTasks` 明确从看板视图中排除子任务：\n  ```tsx\n  let result = tasks.filter(task => !task.parentTaskId);\n  ```\n\n- **`TaskColumn.tsx`**：子任务被重新排序，直接出现在其父任务下方的同一列中，带有视觉缩进：\n  ```tsx\n  {orderedTasks.map(({ task, isSubtask }) => (\n    <div className={`relative ${isSubtask ? \"ml-4 pl-2 border-l-2 border-gray-200\" : \"\"}`}>\n      <TaskCard task={task} ... />\n    </div>\n  ))}\n  ```\n\n**测试：** `src/web/__tests__/subtask-navigation.test.ts` — 8 个测试用例，涵盖 API 成功、本地回退、错误处理和渲染条件。\n\n---\n\n#### 2.4 看板过滤器增强 (BACK-42)\n\n**仅活跃切换：**\n- `INACTIVE_STATUSES = new Set(['done', 'cancelled', 'parked'])`\n- 启用时过滤任务并隐藏匹配的状态列\n- 按钮激活时显示蓝色高亮\n\n**优先级排序：**\n- 切换循环：关闭 → 降序（高→低）→ 升序（低→高）→ 关闭\n- 分组后按状态在每个泳道内应用\n- `PRIORITY_RANK: { high: 0, medium: 1, low: 2 }`\n\n**标签过滤（OR 逻辑，分组）：**\n- 标签按前缀（`dept:`、`type:`、`module:`）分组，带有分隔条\n- 点击切换包含；多个选择使用 OR 逻辑\n- `dept:*` 标签使用部门颜色方案渲染\n- 激活任何过滤器时显示「清除」按钮\n\n**里程碑泳道改进：**\n- 里程碑模式下隐藏空泳道（`visibleLanes` memo）\n- 仅当存在 2+ 个可见泳道时才显示泳道标题\n- 按里程碑过滤时，不匹配的泳道自动折叠\n\n---\n\n#### 2.5 部署文档 (TASK-102)\n\n**新文件：`docs/DEPLOYMENT.md`**\n\n记录他们的生产环境设置：\n- **开发实例** (`psydell`)：通过 `bun src/cli.ts` 从源码运行\n- **生产实例** (`mn-cfo`, Hetzner CAX21 ARM64): `tasks.mensnetwork.global`\n- **技术栈**：Caddy 反向代理 → `oauth2-proxy` (Google OAuth, 仅限 `@mensnetwork.global`) → backlog 二进制文件 `:3001`\n- **交叉编译**：使用 `bun build --target=bun-linux-arm64` 构建 ARM64 二进制文件\n- **Systemd 服务**：`backlog-browser.service`，带有自动重启\n- **CLAUDE.md 已更新**：部署部分简化为引用 `docs/DEPLOYMENT.md`\n\n---\n\n### 总结对比\n\n| 方面 | SkogBackup/backlog | MensNetwork/backlog.md |\n|--------|-------------------|------------------------|\n| **方法** | 剥离与重品牌 | 扩展与自定义 |\n| **代码变更** | 1 个配置文件 + 502 个删除文件 | ~1,500+ 行，跨越 15+ 个文件 |\n| **新功能** | 无（空白画布） | 收件箱、部门颜色、子任务层级、过滤器 |\n| **数据模型** | 未更改 | 复用现有的 `parentTaskId` / `subtaskSummaries` |\n| **服务器 API** | 未更改 | 3 个新的收件箱端点 |\n| **Web UI** | 未更改 | 新的收件箱页面、增强的看板、增强的任务详情 |\n| **测试** | 无 | 8 个子任务导航测试 |\n| **文档** | 删除了大多数模板文档 | 添加了部署参考文档 |\n| **部署** | 不适用 | 生产环境 systemd + Caddy + OAuth2 设置 |\n\n---\n\n## 3. 深度分析：andrewlongman07 — 甘特时间线与调度\n\n**Fork：** [andrewlongman07/Backlog.md](https://github.com/andrewlongman07/Backlog.md)  \n**创建时间：** 2025-12-08 | **最后推送：** 2025-12-17 | **差距：** 9 天  \n**关键功能：** 计划起止日期 + 浏览器甘特图视图\n\n### 3.1 概述\n\n此 Fork 通过两个新任务字段（`plannedStart` / `plannedEnd`）和 Web UI 中完全客户端端的**甘特时间线视图**，为 Backlog.md 添加了**项目规划能力**。与典型的引入 D3 或 Chart.js 等重型图表库的甘特实现不同，此实现是**纯 React + CSS 定位**——零额外依赖。\n\n**更改文件：** 25 个文件，+635 行，-20 行  \n**提交：** `17019fe` — \"add planned start and end dates, added gantt view\"\n\n---\n\n### 3.2 数据模型扩展\n\n**添加到核心类型的新字段 (`src/types/index.ts`)：**\n\n```typescript\nexport interface Task {\n  // ... 现有字段\n  plannedStart?: string;  // \"YYYY-MM-DD\" 或 \"YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm\"\n  plannedEnd?: string;\n}\n\nexport interface TaskCreateInput {\n  // ...\n  plannedStart?: string;\n  plannedEnd?: string;\n}\n\nexport interface TaskUpdateInput {\n  // ...\n  plannedStart?: string;\n  plannedEnd?: string;\n}\n```\n\n**`TaskEditArgs` 和 `task-edit-builder.ts`** 也进行了扩展，使 CLI `--planned-start` / `--planned-end` 选项贯穿整个编辑流程。\n\n---\n\n### 3.3 Markdown 持久化\n\n日期使用 snake_case 键（`planned_start`、`planned_end`）存储在任务 frontmatter 中，并通过现有的 `normalizeDate()` 辅助函数规范化：\n\n**解析器 (`src/markdown/parser.ts`)：**\n```typescript\nplannedStart: frontmatter.planned_start ? normalizeDate(frontmatter.planned_start) : undefined,\nplannedEnd: frontmatter.planned_end ? normalizeDate(frontmatter.planned_end) : undefined,\n```\n\n**序列化器 (`src/markdown/serializer.ts`)：**\n```typescript\n...(task.plannedStart && { planned_start: task.plannedStart }),\n...(task.plannedEnd && { planned_end: task.plannedEnd }),\n```\n\n**测试覆盖：** `src/test/markdown.test.ts` — 验证纯日期和日期时间格式的计划日期的解析 + 往返。\n\n---\n\n### 3.4 CLI 集成\n\n为 `backlog task create` 和 `backlog task edit` 添加了新选项：\n\n```bash\nbacklog task create \"My task\" --planned-start 2025-12-01 --planned-end 2025-12-15\nbacklog task edit TASK-123 --planned-start 2025-12-01\n```\n\n**实现 (`src/cli.ts`)：**\n```typescript\n.option(\"--planned-start <date>\", \"set planned start date (YYYY-MM-DD)\")\n.option(\"--planned-end <date>\", \"set planned end date (YYYY-MM-DD)\")\n```\n\n值通过 `TaskEditArgs` → `buildTaskUpdateInput()` → `Core.updateTask()` → markdown 序列化器流动。\n\n---\n\n### 3.5 服务器 API 和 MCP\n\n**服务器 (`src/server/index.ts`)：**\n- 创建端点将 `plannedStart` / `plannedEnd` 传递给 `Core.createTask()`\n- 更新端点检查请求负载中字符串类型的 `plannedStart` / `plannedEnd`\n\n**MCP (`src/mcp/tools/tasks/handlers.ts` + `src/mcp/utils/schema-generators.ts`)：**\n- `TaskCreateArgs` 和 `TaskListArgs` 扩展了可选日期字段\n- JSON Schema 描述：`\"Optional planned start date (YYYY-MM-DD or YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm)\"`\n- 最大长度验证：25 个字符\n\n---\n\n### 3.6 Web UI — 任务详情弹窗\n\n**`TaskDetailsModal.tsx` 中的新部分** — 「计划日期」可折叠部分下的两个 `type=\"date\"` 输入框：\n\n```tsx\n<input\n  type=\"date\"\n  value={plannedStart}\n  onChange={(e) => {\n    const value = e.target.value;\n    setPlannedStart(value);\n    void handleInlinePlannedDateChange(\"plannedStart\", value);\n  }}\n/>\n```\n\n**内联更新模式：** 更改通过 `apiClient.updateTask()` 立即保存（乐观本地状态 + websocket 驱动的后台刷新）。弹窗不会关闭或触发完整页面重新加载——全局 `tasks` 数组通过现有的 websocket `tasks-updated` 事件更新。\n\n**脏检查：** `plannedStart` 和 `plannedEnd` 包含在 `isDirty` memo 中，因此「保存」按钮状态和「丢弃更改？」提示正常工作。\n\n---\n\n### 3.7 Web UI — GanttPage（核心实现）\n\n**文件：** `src/web/components/GanttPage.tsx`（316 行）  \n**方法：** 零外部图表库。纯 React + Tailwind CSS + 基于百分比的定位。\n\n#### 架构\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  GanttPage                                                  │\n│  ├── useMemo: ganttTasks  (过滤有日期的任务)                │\n│  ├── useMemo: fullRange   (所有任务的最小/最大值)           │\n│  ├── useMemo: range       (基于缩放的视口)                  │\n│  ├── useMemo: ticks       (时间轴标签)                      │\n│  └── 渲染:                                                  │\n│       ├── 缩放控制栏                                        │\n│       ├── 时间轴 (比例刻度标签)                             │\n│       └── 任务行 (标题列 + 条形列)                          │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n#### 1. 任务过滤与规范化\n\n```typescript\nconst ganttTasks: GanttTask[] = useMemo(() => {\n  return tasks\n    .map((task) => {\n      const start = task.plannedStart ? toDate(task.plannedStart) : null;\n      const end = task.plannedEnd ? toDate(task.plannedEnd) : null;\n      if (!start && !end) return null;  // 跳过没有日期的任务\n      const normalizedStart = start ?? end;\n      const normalizedEnd = end ?? start;\n      // ...\n    })\n    .filter((t): t is GanttTask => Boolean(t));\n}, [tasks]);\n```\n\n**优雅处理：** 没有 `plannedStart`/`plannedEnd` 的任务被静默排除。空状态消息解释如何添加日期。\n\n#### 2. 日期解析\n\n```typescript\nfunction toDate(value: string): Date | null {\n  if (!value) return null;\n  const hasTime = value.includes(\" \") || value.includes(\"T\");\n  const iso = value.replace(\" \", \"T\") + (hasTime ? \":00Z\" : \"T00:00:00Z\");\n  const d = new Date(iso);\n  return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;\n}\n```\n\n支持 `YYYY-MM-DD` 和 `YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm` 两种格式。内部规范化为 UTC ISO 字符串。\n\n#### 3. 缩放级别与视口\n\n```typescript\ntype ZoomLevel = \"1d\" | \"7d\" | \"30d\" | \"90d\" | \"365d\" | \"all\";\n```\n\n| 缩放 | 行为 |\n|------|-----------|\n| `all` | 显示从最早任务开始到最后任务结束的完整范围 |\n| `1d` / `7d` / `30d` / `90d` / `365d` | 锚定到「今天」，从当前日期向后/向前显示窗口 |\n\n**视口计算：**\n```typescript\nconst now = Date.now();\nconst start = now - dayMs; // 今天前一天\nconst min = start;\nconst max = min + windowMs;\n```\n\n#### 4. 智能时间轴刻度\n\n刻度密度根据缩放级别调整：\n\n| 缩放 | 刻度步长 | 对齐方式 |\n|------|-----------|-----------|\n| `1d`, `7d` | 每日 | 午夜 |\n| `30d` | 每周 | 周一对齐 |\n| `90d`, `365d`, `all` | 每月 | 每月 1 日 |\n\n```typescript\n// 30d 窗口：对齐到周一\nconst diffToMonday = (day + 6) % 7;\nstartDate.setDate(startDate.getDate() - diffToMonday);\n```\n\n#### 5. 条形定位（核心渲染逻辑）\n\n**无 SVG，无 canvas — 纯 CSS：**\n\n```tsx\n<div className=\"flex-1 relative h-6\">\n  {/* 背景网格线 */}\n  <div className=\"absolute inset-y-2 left-0 right-0 border-t border-dashed\" />\n  \n  {/* 条形 */}\n  <div\n    className={`absolute top-0 bottom-0 rounded-full ${color} shadow-sm`}\n    style={{\n      left: `${startOffset}%`,   // 从视口左侧的百分比\n      width: `${width}%`,        // 视口跨度的百分比\n    }}\n    title={`${formatDay(task.plannedStart)} → ${formatDay(task.plannedEnd)}`}\n  />\n</div>\n```\n\n**定位数学：**\n```typescript\nconst startOffset = ((clampedStart - range.min) / range.span) * 100;\nconst width = Math.max(((clampedEnd - clampedStart) / range.span) * 100, 2);\n// 最小 2% 宽度，因此短任务保持可见\n```\n\n**裁剪：** 超出视口的任务被裁剪：\n```typescript\nconst clampedStart = Math.max(startMs, range.min);\nconst clampedEnd = Math.min(endMs, range.max);\nif (clampedEnd <= range.min || clampedStart >= range.max) return null;\n```\n\n#### 6. 状态颜色\n\n```typescript\nconst color =\n  status.includes(\"done\") || status.includes(\"complete\")\n    ? \"bg-green-500\"\n    : status.includes(\"progress\")\n      ? \"bg-blue-500\"\n      : \"bg-gray-400\";\n```\n\n- **绿色** = 完成 / 已完成\n- **蓝色** = 进行中\n- **灰色** = 默认（待办等）\n\n#### 7. 交互性\n\n- **点击任务行** → 通过 `onEditTask` 回调打开 `TaskDetailsModal`\n- **悬停在条形上** → 原生浏览器工具提示显示日期范围\n- **缩放按钮** → 即时重新渲染，无网络请求\n\n---\n\n### 3.8 导航与路由\n\n**SideNavigation (`src/web/components/SideNavigation.tsx`)：**\n- 新的「Gantt」导航项，带有自定义 SVG 图标（水平条形图形状）\n- 添加到展开的侧边栏和折叠的仅图标模式\n\n**App.tsx 路由：**\n```tsx\n<Route\n  path=\"gantt\"\n  element={<GanttPage tasks={tasks} onEditTask={handleEditTask} />}\n/>\n```\n\nGantt 页面从 App 的状态接收完整的 `tasks` 数组（已通过 websocket 加载），因此导航时无需额外的 API 调用。\n\n---\n\n### 3.9 技术亮点\n\n| 决策 | 理由 |\n|----------|-----------|\n| **无图表库** | 保持包体积小巧；Tailwind CSS 处理所有样式；更易于维护 |\n| **百分比定位** | 无需 ResizeObserver 即可响应；随容器宽度自然重排 |\n| **Task 类型中的日期字符串（非 Date 对象）** | 与现有的 `createdDate` / `updatedDate` 模式一致；易于 markdown 往返 |\n| **内部 UTC 规范化** | 避免时区错误；显示使用 `toLocaleDateString()` 进行用户本地格式化 |\n| **内联日期更新** | 即时反馈；websocket 自动跨客户端同步 |\n| **2% 最小条形宽度** | 防止在宽缩放级别下单日任务消失 |\n| **周一对齐的每周刻度** | 遵循 ISO 周约定；项目规划者熟悉 |\n\n---\n\n### 3.10 与典型甘特实现的对比\n\n| 方面 | 典型 (D3/Chart.js) | andrewlongman07 方法 |\n|--------|----------------------|--------------------------|\n| 依赖 | +1 个重型库 | 零新依赖 |\n| 包体积 | +50-200 KB | ~0 KB 开销 |\n| 渲染 | SVG/Canvas | DOM divs + CSS |\n| 响应性 | 需要 ResizeObserver | 自然 CSS 重排 |\n| 自定义 | 库特定的 API | 标准 React/JSX/Tailwind |\n| 拖拽调整条形 | 复杂的事件处理 | 未实现（点击编辑） |\n| 任务之间的依赖关系 | 箭头绘制 | 未实现 |\n\n**权衡：** 更简单更轻量，但缺乏拖拽调整、依赖箭头或关键路径分析等高级功能。对于轻量级任务追踪器，这是一种务实的平衡。\n\n---\n\n### 3.11 潜在上游价值\n\n此实现**高度可上游化**，因为：\n\n1. **无破坏性变更** — `plannedStart`/`plannedEnd` 在所有地方都是可选字段\n2. **遵循现有模式** — frontmatter 序列化、类型扩展和 CLI 选项模式与代码库约定匹配\n3. **自包含** — GanttPage 是单个组件文件；易于审查和合并\n4. **已测试** — 包含 Markdown 解析/序列化测试\n5. **MCP 就绪** — AI 代理已经可以通过 MCP 工具读取/写入计划日期\n\n**建议的上游改进：**\n- 添加日期验证（在 API 层面拒绝 end < start）\n- 添加逾期高亮（当 `plannedEnd < today && status !== done` 时显示红色色调）\n- 添加拖拽调整条形（需要鼠标事件处理 + 乐观 API 更新）\n- 导出甘特视图为 PNG/SVG 用于报告\n"
}
```

根据之前的分析：

MensNetwork 的收件箱系统是一个完整的 Gmail 风格的双面板消息 UI，添加到了 Web UI 中。

核心设计：
1. **文件系统约定**：在 backlog 目录下创建 departments/{ceo,cto,cmo,cfo}/inbox/ 和 modules/{name}/inbox/ 目录结构
2. **消息格式**：Markdown 文件，YAML frontmatter 包含 title, from, to, type, priority, date 等字段
3. **服务器 API**：3 个新端点
   - GET /api/inbox - 扫描目录返回消息列表
   - GET /api/inbox/message?path= - 读取单条消息
   - POST /api/inbox/archive - 归档消息（移动到 processed/ 子目录）
4. **客户端 UI**：InboxPage.tsx（501 行）
   - 左侧面板：消息列表（未读指示、部门徽章、模块徽章、预览）
   - 右侧面板：消息详情（元数据、标记已读/未读、归档按钮）
   - 工具栏：部门过滤、模块过滤、显示已归档
   - 读取状态：localStorage 持久化，5 秒自动标记已读
5. **导航集成**：SideNavigation 添加了收件箱项，带未读计数徽章

让我用自然语言详细解释这个系统。

</details>

MensNetwork 的**收件箱系统**是一个在 Backlog.md Web UI 中内嵌的完整消息/通知系统，采用 **Gmail 风格的双面板布局**。它不是传统意义上的邮件系统，而是一个基于 Markdown 文件的**部门级消息路由**机制。

---

## 核心设计

### 1. 文件系统约定

消息以 Markdown 文件的形式存放在 backlog 目录的特定位置：

```
{c-suite-root}/
├── departments/
│   ├── ceo/inbox/          ← CEO 的收件箱
│   ├── cto/inbox/          ← CTO 的收件箱
│   ├── cmo/inbox/          ← CMO 的收件箱
│   └── cfo/inbox/          ← CFO 的收件箱
└── modules/
    └── {module-name}/inbox/  ← 各业务模块的收件箱
```

**归档机制**：处理后的消息会被移动到对应目录的 `processed/` 子文件夹中。

### 2. 消息格式

每条消息就是一个带 YAML frontmatter 的 Markdown 文件：

```yaml
---
title: "Q2 预算审批申请"
from: "cto@company.com"
to: "cfo@company.com"
type: "memo"
priority: "high"
date: "2026-04-07"
---

请审阅附件中的 Q2 技术预算方案...
```

---

## 服务器端 API（3 个新端点）

| 端点 | 方法 | 功能 |
|------|------|------|
| `GET /api/inbox` | 扫描 | 遍历所有 `departments/*/inbox` 和 `modules/*/inbox` 目录，返回消息列表 |
| `GET /api/inbox/message?path=` | 读取 | 读取单条消息的 Markdown 内容（带路径遍历安全检查，防止 `../` 攻击） |
| `POST /api/inbox/archive` | 归档 | 将消息文件移动到 `processed/` 子目录 |

---

## 客户端 UI（InboxPage.tsx，501 行）

### 左侧面板 — 消息列表
- 每条消息显示：**未读蓝点** + **部门/模块徽章** + **发件人** + **日期** + **内容预览**
- 部门徽章带颜色编码：CEO（深蓝）、CTO（深绿）、CMO（深紫）、CFO（深橙）
- 点击选中后高亮显示

### 右侧面板 — 消息详情
- 标题 + 元数据行（发件人/收件人/优先级/日期）
- **标记已读/未读** 切换按钮
- **归档**按钮（点击后将文件移到 `processed/`）
- 已归档的消息会显示「已归档」状态徽章
- 正文通过 `MermaidMarkdown` 组件渲染（支持 Mermaid 图表）

### 顶部工具栏过滤器
- **部门标签**：全部 | CEO | CTO | CMO | CFO
- **模块胶囊按钮**：动态发现所有模块
- **「显示已归档」** 复选框
- **未读计数徽章**

### 读取状态管理
- 用 `localStorage`（键：`backlog:inbox:read`）持久化已读记录
- 选中一条消息后，**5 秒后自动标记为已读**
- 未读计数会实时反映在侧边栏导航上

---

## 与导航的集成

**SideNavigation** 新增了一个「收件箱」导航项：
- 信封图标
- 右上角显示未读数量（上限「9+」）
- 展开/折叠两种侧边栏模式下都有徽章

---

## 这个系统的本质

它不是一个独立的邮件客户端，而是**把 Backlog.md 当作了一个组织内部的轻量级消息中枢**：
- 各部门/模块的收件箱就是目录
- 消息就是带元数据的 Markdown 文件
- 归档就是文件移动
- 读取状态存在浏览器本地

这种设计与 Backlog.md「一切用 Markdown 管理」的哲学完全一致，没有引入数据库或外部消息服务，纯粹基于文件系统。

- 优点：简单，不需要数据库，不需要用户认证系统，完全基于文件

## 安全模型分析

### 1. 完全透明，没有隐私隔离

```
departments/
├── ceo/inbox/secret-strategy.md     ← CEO 的「机密策略」
├── cto/inbox/hiring-plan.md         ← CTO 的「招聘计划」
├── cfo/inbox/salary-adjustments.md  ← CFO 的「薪资调整」
└── cmo/inbox/campaign-budget.md     ← CMO 的「活动预算」
```

在文件系统层面，**这些目录没有任何访问控制**。只要你能访问 backlog 项目的根目录：
- `cat departments/ceo/inbox/secret-strategy.md` 就能直接读取
- `mv departments/ceo/inbox/xxx.md processed/` 就能直接归档

### 2. Web UI 层面也没有权限检查

**API 端点：**
- `GET /api/inbox` — 遍历**所有**部门/模块的 inbox，返回完整列表
- `GET /api/inbox/message?path=ceo/inbox/xxx.md` — 读取**任意**消息
- `POST /api/inbox/archive` — 归档**任意**消息

代码里只有一个**路径遍历安全检查**（防止 `../../../etc/passwd` 这种攻击），但没有「你是谁、你能看哪个部门」的认证逻辑。

### 3. 读取状态更是纯本地的

未读/已读状态存在**浏览器 localStorage** 里（`backlog:inbox:read`），不是服务器端的状态。这意味着：
- 张三在 Chrome 上标记为已读，李四在 Safari 上打开还是未读
- 换台电脑/清除浏览器数据，所有已读状态就丢了

---

## MensNetwork 怎么解决这个问题的？

他们并没有在应用层解决，而是在**基础设施层**做了防护：

```
用户 → Caddy → oauth2-proxy (Google OAuth, 仅限 @mensnetwork.global 域名)
                                               ↓
                                      backlog-browser :3001
```

也就是说——**任何人只要进入了这个网站，就能看到所有消息**。认证只控制「谁能打开网站」，不控制「谁能看哪条消息」。

---

## 为什么是这种设计？

这不是疏忽，而是 Backlog.md 哲学的一种极端体现：

| 设计选择 | 理由 |
|----------|------|
| 没有用户系统 | Backlog.md 本身没有用户/认证概念，全是文件 |
| 没有权限模型 | 保持与现有任务文件一致的「全员可见」模式 |
| 归档 = 文件移动 | 复用了文件系统的原子操作，不需要数据库事务 |
| localStorage 存已读 | 简单、无服务器状态、离线可用 |

**一句话总结：** 这是一个「信任网络」模型——假设所有能访问 backlog 仓库的人都是组织内部可信成员，不需要部门间隔离。

---

## 潜在风险

如果你在自己的团队里用类似方案，需要注意：

1. **fork 出去的外部协作者**能看到所有历史消息
2. **任何人误点归档**就会把消息移到 processed/，没有撤销机制
3. **没有审计日志**——谁读了、谁归档了，完全无记录
4. **消息内容没有加密**——仓库被 clone 到本地就全部暴露

这在 MensNetwork 的场景下可能够用（小而封闭的团队 + Google Workspace 域名限制），但不太适合需要部门间保密或外部协作的场景。
